=== backend\app.py ===

import sys, io
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')
sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')

import os, shutil, uuid, time, threading, subprocess, queue, logging, mimetypes, sqlite3, bcrypt, secrets
from flask import Flask, request, send_file, render_template, Response, jsonify, make_response
from flask_cors import CORS, cross_origin
from pydub import AudioSegment
from pydub.utils import mediainfo
from pathlib import Path
from audio_splitter import split_audio
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from werkzeug.exceptions import RequestEntityTooLarge
from werkzeug.utils import secure_filename
from werkzeug.security import generate_password_hash, check_password_hash
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from disposable_email_domains import blocklist
from datetime import datetime, timedelta


app = Flask(__name__)
CORS(app, resources={r"/*": {"origins": "*"}}, supports_credentials=True)

load_dotenv()
def get_env_int(name, default):
    try:
        return int(os.getenv(name, default))
    except:
        return default
    
MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH_MB = int(os.getenv("MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH", 100))
MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH = MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH_MB * 1024 * 1024
MAX_DISK_USAGE_MB = int(os.getenv("MAX_DISK_USAGE_MB", 1000))
MAX_JOBS_PER_IP = int(os.getenv("MAX_JOBS_PER_IP", 2))
MAX_UPLOADS_PER_MIN = int(os.getenv("MAX_UPLOADS_PER_MIN", 5))
TEMP_DIR = os.getenv("TEMP_DIR", "temp")
LOG_FILE = os.getenv("LOG_FILE", "logs/app.log")
JOB_EXPIRATION = int(os.getenv("JOB_EXPIRATION", 300))  # fallback 5 min



ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {".mp3", ".wav", ".flac", ".m4a", ".aac", ".ogg", ".aiff", ".mp4"}

app.config["MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH"] = MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH_MB * 1024 * 1024
os.makedirs(TEMP_DIR, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(LOG_FILE), exist_ok=True)

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s]: %(message)s",
                    handlers=[logging.FileHandler(LOG_FILE), logging.StreamHandler()])
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

JOBS = {}
UPLOAD_HISTORY = defaultdict(lambda: deque())
active_processing = set()
active_downloading = set()
jobs_lock = threading.Lock()

def get_db_connection():
    BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    db_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "db", "users.db")
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn

def allowed_file(filename):
    return filename and Path(filename).suffix.lower() in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS

def log(job_id, msg):
    logger.info(f"[{job_id}] {msg}")
    if job_id in JOBS:
        JOBS[job_id]["events"].put(msg)

def startup_cleanup():
    logger.info("Running startup cleanup of TEMP_DIR...")
    for item in os.listdir(TEMP_DIR):
        path = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, item)
        try:
            if os.path.isdir(path):
                shutil.rmtree(path, ignore_errors=True)
            else:
                os.remove(path)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Startup cleanup error on {path}: {e}")

def cleanup_old_temp():
    try:
        total_size = 0
        folder_stats = []
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(TEMP_DIR):
            for f in files:
                total_size += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, f))
            for d in dirs:
                folder_path = os.path.join(root, d)
                folder_size = sum(os.path.getsize(os.path.join(dp, file))
                                  for dp, dn, filenames in os.walk(folder_path) for file in filenames)
                last_modified = os.path.getmtime(folder_path)
                folder_stats.append((folder_path, folder_size, last_modified))

        if total_size / (1024 * 1024) > MAX_DISK_USAGE_MB:
            logger.warning("Disk usage exceeded, purging old temp folders...")
            for folder_path, _, _ in sorted(folder_stats, key=lambda x: (x[2], x[1])):
                folder_uuid = os.path.basename(folder_path)
                if folder_uuid in active_processing or folder_uuid in active_downloading:
                    continue
                shutil.rmtree(folder_path, ignore_errors=True)
                logger.info(f"Purged job folder: {folder_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Cleanup error: {e}")

def process_job(job_id, upload_path, filename, selected_stems):
    start_time = time.time()
    try:
        log(job_id, "Starting Demucs split...")

        demucs_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, "stems")
        preview_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, "preview")
        os.makedirs(preview_dir, exist_ok=True)
        base_name = os.path.splitext(filename)[0]
        zip_output_path = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, f"{base_name}_stems.zip")

        use_two_stems = sorted(selected_stems) == ["other", "vocals"]
        track_dir = split_audio(
            upload_path,
            output_dir=demucs_dir,
            stem_type="2stems" if use_two_stems else "all",
            job_id=job_id,
            logger=logger
        )

        all_possible_stems = {"vocals", "drums", "bass", "other", "no_vocals"}
        for stem in all_possible_stems:
            if stem == "no_vocals":
                if "other" in selected_stems:
                    continue
            elif stem not in selected_stems:
                file_path = os.path.join(track_dir, f"{stem}.wav")
                if os.path.exists(file_path):
                    os.remove(file_path)

        for stem in selected_stems:
            src = os.path.join(track_dir, f"{stem}.wav")
            if stem == "other" and not os.path.exists(src):
                fallback = os.path.join(track_dir, "no_vocals.wav")
                if os.path.exists(fallback):
                    src = fallback
            if os.path.exists(src):
                dst_preview = os.path.join(preview_dir, f"{stem}.mp3")
                audio = AudioSegment.from_wav(src)
                export_opts = {"format": "mp3", "bitrate": "192k"}
                if len(audio) > 30_000:
                    audio[:30_000].export(dst_preview, **export_opts)
                else:
                    audio.export(dst_preview, **export_opts)

        shutil.make_archive(zip_output_path.replace(".zip", ""), 'zip', track_dir)

        try:
            os.remove(upload_path)
        except Exception:
            pass
        shutil.rmtree(demucs_dir, ignore_errors=True)

        with jobs_lock:
            JOBS[job_id]["status"] = "done"
            JOBS[job_id]["zip"] = zip_output_path

        duration = time.time() - start_time
        log(job_id, f"Processing time: {duration:.2f} seconds")
        log(job_id, "Job complete. ZIP ready.")
        JOBS[job_id]["events"].put("Job complete")

        def auto_cleanup():
            time.sleep(JOB_EXPIRATION)
            with jobs_lock:
                if job_id in JOBS and JOBS[job_id]["status"] == "done":
                    folder = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id)
                    shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)
                    JOBS.pop(job_id, None)
                    logger.info(f"[CLEANUP] Auto-deleted job {job_id}")

        threading.Thread(target=auto_cleanup, daemon=True).start()

    except Exception as e:
        with jobs_lock:
            if job_id in JOBS:
                JOBS[job_id]["status"] = "error"
                JOBS[job_id]["zip"] = None
        log(job_id, f"Error: {e}")
    finally:
        active_processing.discard(job_id)



def is_rate_limited(ip):
    now = time.time()
    window = 60
    max_uploads = 2  # Your .env value

    with jobs_lock:
        history = UPLOAD_HISTORY[ip]

        # Remove expired timestamps
        while history and now - history[0] > window:
            history.popleft()

        # ✅ BLOCK if over frequency limit
        if len(history) >= max_uploads:
            return f"Upload rate limit exceeded ({max_uploads} per {window}s)."

        # ✅ Count this attempt no matter what
        history.append(now)

        # ✅ Then check active jobs
        active_jobs = sum(
            1 for job in JOBS.values()
            if job.get("ip") == ip and job.get("status") == "processing"
        )
        if active_jobs >= MAX_JOBS_PER_IP:
            return f"Too many jobs processing (limit: {MAX_JOBS_PER_IP})."

    return None


@app.route("/upload", methods=["POST"])
@cross_origin()
def upload():
    cleanup_old_temp()
    ip = request.remote_addr
    rate_limit_msg = is_rate_limited(ip)
    if rate_limit_msg:
        return jsonify({"error": rate_limit_msg}), 429

    if "file" not in request.files:
        return jsonify({"error": "No file uploaded."}), 400

    file = request.files["file"]
    filename = secure_filename(file.filename)
    if not filename or not allowed_file(filename):
        return jsonify({"error": "Unsupported file type."}), 400

    # 👇 Calculate file size
    file.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)
    file_size_mb = round(file.tell() / (1024 * 1024), 2)
    file.seek(0)

    # 👇 Check account status
    email = request.headers.get("X-User-Email")
    user_id = None
    is_guest = True
    db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")

    if email:
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
            c = conn.cursor()
            c.execute("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
            result = c.fetchone()
            if result:
                user_id = result[0]
                is_guest = False
        except Exception as e:
            print("Failed to look up user:", e)
        finally:
            if conn:
                conn.close()

    # 👇 Usage check
    if is_guest:
        weekly_usage = get_guest_weekly_usage(ip)
        cap = 150
    else:
        weekly_usage = get_account_weekly_usage(user_id)
        cap = 500

    if weekly_usage + file_size_mb > cap:
        return jsonify({
            "error": f"Weekly limit exceeded ({cap}MB max). You’ve used {round(weekly_usage, 2)}MB."
        }), 403

    selected_stems = request.form.getlist("stems") or ["vocals", "other"]
    job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    active_processing.add(job_id)

    temp_upload_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id)
    os.makedirs(temp_upload_dir, exist_ok=True)
    upload_path = os.path.join(temp_upload_dir, filename)
    file.save(upload_path)

    try:
        duration = float(mediainfo(upload_path).get("duration", "0"))
        if duration <= 0:
            raise ValueError("No valid audio stream")
        if duration > 600:
            raise ValueError("File too long")
    except Exception:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_upload_dir, ignore_errors=True)
        active_processing.discard(job_id)
        return jsonify({"error": "Invalid or corrupted audio file."}), 400

    if Path(filename).suffix.lower() == ".mp4":
        wav_path = os.path.join(temp_upload_dir, f"{Path(filename).stem}.wav")
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-i", upload_path, "-vn", "-acodec", "pcm_s16le", wav_path, "-y"]
        log(job_id, f"Extracting audio from MP4: {' '.join(cmd)}")
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
        if result.returncode != 0 or not os.path.exists(wav_path) or os.path.getsize(wav_path) < 4096:
            shutil.rmtree(temp_upload_dir, ignore_errors=True)
            active_processing.discard(job_id)
            return jsonify({"error": "Invalid or corrupted audio file."}), 400
        upload_path = wav_path
        log(job_id, "Extracted audio from MP4.")

    # 👇 Log usage to DB
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()
        upload_date = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO usage (user_id, ip, date, mb_used) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (user_id, ip, upload_date, file_size_mb)
        )
        conn.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        print("Failed to log usage:", e)
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()

    with jobs_lock:
        JOBS[job_id] = {"status": "processing", "zip": None, "events": queue.Queue(), "ip": ip}

    threading.Thread(target=process_job, args=(job_id, upload_path, filename, selected_stems)).start()
    return jsonify({"job_id": job_id})



@app.route("/stream/<job_id>")
@cross_origin()
def stream(job_id):
    def event_stream():
        q = JOBS.get(job_id, {}).get("events")
        if not q:
            yield "data: Job not found\n\n"
            return
        yield "data: Processing started...\n\n"
        while True:
            msg = q.get()
            yield f"data: {msg}\n\n"
            if "Job complete" in msg or "Error" in msg:
                break
    return Response(event_stream(), mimetype="text/event-stream")

@app.route("/download/<job_id>")
@cross_origin()
def download(job_id):
    job = JOBS.get(job_id)
    zip_path = job["zip"] if job else None

    if not job or not zip_path or not os.path.exists(zip_path):
        return jsonify({"error": "Download link expired. Please re-upload your file."}), 410

    temp_zip_path = zip_path + ".tmp"
    shutil.move(zip_path, temp_zip_path)
    folder_to_delete = os.path.dirname(temp_zip_path)
    active_downloading.add(job_id)

    with jobs_lock:
        JOBS.pop(job_id, None)

    filename = os.path.basename(zip_path)
    response = make_response(send_file(temp_zip_path, as_attachment=True))
    response.headers["Content-Type"] = mimetypes.guess_type(temp_zip_path)[0] or "application/octet-stream"
    response.headers["Content-Disposition"] = f'attachment; filename="{filename}"'
    response.headers["Access-Control-Expose-Headers"] = "Content-Disposition"

    def cleanup_job():
        time.sleep(2)
        active_downloading.discard(job_id)
        shutil.rmtree(folder_to_delete, ignore_errors=True)
        logger.info(f"Cleaned up temp folder: {folder_to_delete}")

    threading.Thread(target=cleanup_job).start()
    return response


@app.route("/preview/<job_id>/<stem>")
@cross_origin()
def preview(job_id, stem):
    preview_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, "preview")
    if not os.path.exists(preview_dir):
        return jsonify({"error": "Preview not found or expired."}), 404

    path = os.path.join(preview_dir, f"{stem}.mp3")
    if stem == "other" and not os.path.exists(path):
        fallback = os.path.join(preview_dir, "no_vocals.wav")
        if os.path.exists(fallback):
            path = fallback

    if os.path.exists(path):
        return send_file(path, mimetype="audio/mpeg")
    return jsonify({"error": "Stem not found."}), 404


# SIGNUP
@app.route("/signup", methods=["POST"])
def signup():
    data = request.get_json()
    username = data.get("username")
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    if not username or not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"error": "All fields are required."}), 400

    # Check for disposable email
    domain = email.split("@")[-1]
    if domain in blocklist:
        return jsonify({"error": "Disposable email addresses are not allowed."}), 400

    conn = get_db_connection()
    c = conn.cursor()

    # Check if IP has signed up in last 7 days // COMMENTING OUT JUST FOR TESTING LOCALLY 
    ip = request.remote_addr
    #c.execute('''
    #    SELECT * FROM signup_attempts
    #    WHERE ip_address = ? AND attempted_at >= datetime('now', '-7 days')
    #''', (ip,))
    #if c.fetchone():
    #    conn.close()
    #    return jsonify({"error": "Too many signups from this IP. Try again in 7 days."}), 429

    # Check if email or username already exists
    c.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ? OR username = ?", (email, username))
    if c.fetchone():
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "Email or username already in use."}), 409

    # Create user with is_verified = 0
    hashed_pw = generate_password_hash(password)
    c.execute("INSERT INTO users (username, email, password, is_verified) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)",
              (username, email, hashed_pw))
    user_id = c.lastrowid

    # Insert into signup_attempts
    c.execute("INSERT INTO signup_attempts (ip_address, attempted_at) VALUES (?, datetime('now'))", (ip,))

    # Generate token
    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    expires_at = (datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=24)).isoformat()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO verification_tokens (user_id, token, expires_at, used)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)
    ''', (user_id, token, expires_at))

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

    # Normally you'd send email here
    print(f"DEBUG: Email verification link → http://localhost:3000/verify-email/{token}")

    return jsonify({
    "user": username,
    "verified": False
    }), 200

# LOGIN
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
@cross_origin()
def login():
    data = request.json
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"error": "Email and password required."}), 400

    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()
        c.execute("SELECT password, is_verified FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
        result = c.fetchone()
        conn.close()

        if not result:
            return jsonify({"error": "Invalid credentials."}), 401

        stored_pw, is_verified = result

        if not check_password_hash(stored_pw, password):
            return jsonify({"error": "Invalid credentials."}), 401

        if not is_verified:
            return jsonify({"error": "Please verify your email before logging in."}), 403

        return jsonify({"success": True}), 200

    except Exception as e:
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        return jsonify({"error": f"Server error: {str(e)}"}), 500

@app.route("/verify-email/<token>", methods=["GET"])
@cross_origin()
def verify_email(token):
    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()

        # Find token
        c.execute("SELECT user_id, expires_at, used FROM verification_tokens WHERE token = ?", (token,))
        result = c.fetchone()

        if not result:
            conn.close()
            return jsonify({"error": "Invalid or expired token."}), 400

        user_id, expires_at, used = result

        if used:
            conn.close()
            return jsonify({"error": "This token has already been used."}), 400

        if datetime.utcnow() > datetime.fromisoformat(expires_at):
            conn.close()
            return jsonify({"error": "Token has expired."}), 400

        # Mark user as verified
        c.execute("UPDATE users SET is_verified = 1 WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))
        c.execute("UPDATE verification_tokens SET used = 1 WHERE token = ?", (token,))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

        return jsonify({"success": True, "message": "Email verified successfully."}), 200

    except Exception as e:
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        return jsonify({"error": f"Server error: {str(e)}"}), 500
    
@app.route("/reset-password-request", methods=["POST"])
def reset_password_request():
    data = request.get_json()
    email = data.get("email")

    if not email:
        return jsonify({"error": "Email is required."}), 400

    conn = get_db_connection()
    c = conn.cursor()

    # Check if user exists
    c.execute("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
    user = c.fetchone()
    if not user:
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "Email not found."}), 404

    user_id = user[0]

    # Create reset token
    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    expires_at = (datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat()

    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO password_reset_tokens (user_id, token, expires_at, used)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)
    ''', (user_id, token, expires_at))

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

    # Normally send email
    print(f"DEBUG: Password reset link → http://localhost:3000/reset-password/{token}")

    return jsonify({
        "message": "Password reset link sent to your email.",
        "success": True
    }), 200


    
@app.route("/request-password-reset", methods=["POST"])
def request_password_reset():
    data = request.get_json()
    email = data.get("email")

    if not email:
        return jsonify({"error": "Email is required."}), 400

    conn = get_db_connection()
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute("SELECT id, is_verified FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
    user = c.fetchone()

    if not user:
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "No user found with that email."}), 404

    if user["is_verified"] == 0:
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "Please verify your email before resetting password."}), 403

    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    expires_at = (datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat()

    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO password_reset_tokens (user_id, token, expires_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?)
    ''', (user["id"], token, expires_at))

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

    print(f"DEBUG: Password reset link → http://localhost:3000/reset-password/{token}")
    return jsonify({"message": "Reset link sent (check console)."}), 200

@app.route("/reset-password/<token>", methods=["POST"])
def reset_password(token):
    data = request.get_json()
    new_password = data.get("password")

    if not new_password or len(new_password) < 6:
        return jsonify({"error": "Password must be at least 6 characters."}), 400

    conn = get_db_connection()
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT prt.id, prt.user_id, prt.expires_at, prt.used
        FROM password_reset_tokens prt
        WHERE prt.token = ?
    ''', (token,))
    record = c.fetchone()

    if not record:
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "Invalid or expired token."}), 400

    if record["used"]:
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "This reset link has already been used."}), 400

    if datetime.utcnow() > datetime.fromisoformat(record["expires_at"]):
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "This reset link has expired."}), 400

    hashed_pw = generate_password_hash(new_password)

    # Update user's password
    c.execute("UPDATE users SET password = ? WHERE id = ?", (hashed_pw, record["user_id"]))
    c.execute("UPDATE password_reset_tokens SET used = 1 WHERE id = ?", (record["id"],))

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

    return jsonify({"message": "Password reset successful."}), 200

    
def get_guest_weekly_usage(ip):
    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()

        start_of_week = datetime.utcnow().date() - timedelta(days=datetime.utcnow().weekday())
        start_of_week_str = start_of_week.isoformat()

        c.execute("""
            SELECT SUM(mb_used) FROM usage
            WHERE ip = ? AND user_id IS NULL AND date >= ?
        """, (ip, start_of_week_str))

        result = c.fetchone()[0]
        return result or 0
    except Exception as e:
        print("Failed to fetch guest usage:", e)
        return 0
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()
    
def get_account_weekly_usage(user_id):
    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")

        c.execute("""
            SELECT SUM(mb_used) FROM usage
            WHERE user_id = ? AND date >= ?
        """, (user_id, start_of_week_str))

        result = c.fetchone()[0]
        return result or 0
    except Exception as e:
        print("Failed to fetch account usage:", e)
        return 0
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()

    
@app.route("/usage", methods=["GET"])
@cross_origin()
def usage():
    ip = request.remote_addr
    email = request.headers.get("X-User-Email")
    is_guest = True
    user_id = None
    limit = 150
    used = 0

    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()

        start_of_week = datetime.utcnow().date() - timedelta(days=datetime.utcnow().weekday())
        start_of_week_str = start_of_week.isoformat()

        if email:
            c.execute("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
            result = c.fetchone()
            if result:
                user_id = result[0]
                is_guest = False

        if is_guest:
            c.execute("""
                SELECT SUM(mb_used) FROM usage
                WHERE ip = ? AND user_id IS NULL AND date >= ?
            """, (ip, start_of_week_str))
            limit = 150
        else:
            c.execute("""
                SELECT SUM(mb_used) FROM usage
                WHERE user_id = ? AND date >= ?
            """, (user_id, start_of_week_str))
            limit = 500  # Future: check is_premium flag

        used = c.fetchone()[0] or 0

        return jsonify({
            "used_mb": round(used, 2),
            "limit_mb": limit
        })

    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"error": "Failed to fetch usage"}), 500
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()



@app.errorhandler(RequestEntityTooLarge)
def handle_large_file(e):
    return jsonify({"error": f"File too large. Max {MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH_MB}MB."}), 413

@app.errorhandler(Exception)
def handle_exception(e):
    logger.error(f"Unhandled error: {e}")
    return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error."}), 500

startup_cleanup()

@app.route("/", defaults={"path": ""})
@app.route("/<path:path>")
def serve_react(path):
    from flask import send_from_directory
    if path != "" and os.path.exists(f"../frontend/dist/{path}"):
        return send_from_directory("../frontend/dist", path)
    else:
        return send_from_directory("../frontend/dist", "index.html")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run(debug=True, threaded=True)

=== backend\audio_splitter.py ===


"""
audio_splitter.py
-----------------
Handles audio separation using Demucs.
Runs Demucs completely silent except for start and completion logs.
"""

import subprocess
import os

def split_audio(input_path, output_dir="separated", stem_type="all", job_id=None, logger=None, model_name="htdemucs"):
    """
    Split an audio file into stems using Demucs silently.

    Args:
        input_path (str): Path to the audio file to split.
        output_dir (str): Directory where separated stems are saved.
        stem_type (str): Type of separation ('all' or '2stems').
        job_id (str): Optional job ID for log prefixing.
        logger (logging.Logger): Optional Flask logger.

    Returns:
        str: Path to the directory containing separated stems.
    """
def split_audio(input_path, output_dir="separated", stem_type="all", job_id=None, logger=None):
    """
    Split an audio file into stems using Demucs silently.

    Args:
        input_path (str): Path to the audio file to split.
        output_dir (str): Directory where separated stems are saved.
        stem_type (str): Type of separation ('all' or '2stems').
        job_id (str): Optional job ID for log prefixing.
        logger (logging.Logger): Optional Flask logger.

    Returns:
        str: Path to the directory containing separated stems.
    """
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    model_args = []

    if stem_type == "2stems":
        model_args = ["--two-stems", "vocals"]

    command = ["demucs", input_path, "-o", output_dir] + model_args

    if logger:
        logger.info(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process starting...")
    else:
        print(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process starting...")

    subprocess.run(command, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)

    if logger:
        logger.info(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process complete.")
    else:
        print(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process complete.")

    base_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(input_path))[0]
    model_output_dir = os.listdir(output_dir)[0]
    output_path = os.path.join(output_dir, model_output_dir, base_name)

    if logger:
        logger.info(f"[{job_id}] Demucs wrote to: {os.path.join(output_dir, model_output_dir)}")

    return output_path

=== backend\db\init_db.py ===

import sqlite3
import os

BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
db_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "users.db")

conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
c = conn.cursor()

# Users table
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        password TEXT NOT NULL,
        is_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0
    )
''')

# Usage table
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS usage (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        user_id INTEGER,
        ip TEXT,
        date TEXT,
        mb_used INTEGER
    )
''')

# Verification tokens table
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS verification_tokens (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        token TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        expires_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
        used BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,
        FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
    )
''')

# Signup attempts table
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS signup_attempts (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        ip_address TEXT NOT NULL,
        attempted_at DATETIME NOT NULL
    )
''')

#  Password reset tokens table 
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS password_reset_tokens (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        token TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
    )
''')

conn.commit()
conn.close()

=== backend\utils\email_utils.py ===

import smtplib
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart
import os

def send_email(to_email, subject, html_content):
    msg = MIMEMultipart()
    msg['From'] = os.getenv("MAIL_FROM")
    msg['To'] = to_email
    msg['Subject'] = subject

    msg.attach(MIMEText(html_content, 'html'))

    with smtplib.SMTP_SSL(os.getenv("MAIL_SERVER"), int(os.getenv("MAIL_PORT"))) as server:
        server.login(os.getenv("MAIL_USERNAME"), os.getenv("MAIL_PASSWORD"))
        server.send_message(msg)
        print(f"✅ Email sent to {to_email}")

## 📄 INSTRUCTIONS.md

# EchoMind - Running & Testing Guide

## 🚀 Running the Application (Development)
1. **Activate your virtual environment**:
   ```bash
   # Windows
   venv\Scripts\activate
   # Mac/Linux
   source venv/bin/activate
   ```

2. **Start the Flask backend**:
   ```bash
   cd backend
   python app.py
   ```

3. **Start the React frontend**:
   ```bash
   cd frontend
   npm run dev
   ```

4. **Open your browser at the React URL** (usually):
   ```
   http://localhost:5173
   ```

✅ React handles the UI, Flask handles API routes (`/upload`, `/stream/<job_id>`, `/download/<job_id>`). Both must run at the same time in development.

---

## ✅ Running Automated Tests (Backend)
1. **Enable Test Mode**:
   ```powershell
   # Windows PowerShell
   set ECHO_TEST_MODE=1
   # Mac/Linux
   export ECHO_TEST_MODE=1
   ```

2. **Start the backend in test mode**:
   ```bash
   python app.py
   ```

3. **In another terminal, run the tester**:
   ```bash
   python tests/test_backend_v2.py
   ```

4. **View reports** in `tests/reports/`.

---

## 🛠 Deployment (Production)
1. **Install backend dependencies**:
   ```bash
   pip install -r requirements.txt
   ```

2. **Build React frontend**:
   ```bash
   cd frontend
   npm run build
   ```

3. **Serve built files with Flask or Nginx**:
   - Copy `frontend/dist` into a static folder or configure Flask to serve it.
   - Start backend with Gunicorn:
     ```bash
     gunicorn -w 4 app:app
     ```

4. (Optional) Use Nginx as a reverse proxy for SSL and static assets.

---

## 📂 Project Structure
```
EchoMind/
  backend/
    app.py              # Main Flask backend
    audio_splitter.py   # Demucs audio splitting logic
    requirements.txt    # Backend dependencies
    logs/               # Backend logs (contents ignored in snapshots)
    temp/               # Temporary job files
    uploads/            # Uploaded files
  frontend/
    index.html          # Vite React entry point
    package.json        # Frontend dependencies
    tailwind.config.js  # Tailwind setup
    src/
      api.js            # Axios API calls
      App.jsx           # Main React component/UI logic
      main.jsx          # React entry point
      index.css         # Global styles
  tests/
    test_backend_v2.py  # Automated backend tester
    reports/            # Test reports (contents ignored in snapshots)
```

---

## 🔑 Notes:
- `@cross_origin()` is enabled on `/upload`, `/stream`, `/download` routes for React ↔ Flask communication.
- React URL (`localhost:5173`) and Flask URL (`127.0.0.1:5000`) must both be running during development.
- For production, React is built and served from Flask or a reverse proxy like Nginx.
- `logs/`, `temp/`, and `tests/reports/` are kept for context but their file contents are ignored in snapshots.

## 📄 snapshot_project.py
import os

BASE_DIR = os.getcwd()
OUTPUT_FILE = "clean_snapshot.txt"

# Core files to always include fully
# Core files to always include fully
CORE_FILES = {
    # Backend core
    "backend/app.py",
    "backend/audio_splitter.py",
    "backend/requirements.txt",
    "backend/db/init_db.py",        # DB init script
    "backend/db/users.db",          # SQLite DB file
    "backend/test.mp3",             # Test media (so I see what you’re using)

    # Frontend core
    "frontend/src/api.js",
    "frontend/src/App.jsx",
    "frontend/src/main.jsx",
    "frontend/src/index.css",
    "frontend/src/components/Navbar.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Hero.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/UploadForm.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Faq.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Login.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Signup.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Dashboard.jsx",
    "frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx",
    "frontend/index.html",
    "frontend/package.json",
    "frontend/tailwind.config.js",
    "frontend/postcss.config.js",

    # Project meta
    "INSTRUCTIONS.md",
    "packages_list.txt",
    ".env",

    # Utility / scripts
    "snapshot_project.py",
    "clean_snapshot.txt"
}


# Folders to show but NOT list contents
SKIP_LISTING = {
    os.path.join("backend", "logs"),
    os.path.join("backend", "temp"),
    os.path.join("backend", "uploads"),
    os.path.join("tests", "reports")
}

IGNORE_DIRS = {"node_modules", "venv", "__pycache__"}

with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
    out.write("# 📂 Project Structure\n")

    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(BASE_DIR):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
        rel_root = os.path.relpath(root, BASE_DIR)

        out.write(f"\n# 📁 {rel_root}\n")

        if any(rel_root.startswith(skip) for skip in SKIP_LISTING):
            out.write("  [Contents skipped]\n")
            continue

        for file in files:
            rel_path = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(rel_root, file))
            rel_path_unix = rel_path.replace("\\", "/")

            if rel_path_unix in CORE_FILES:
                out.write(f"\n## 📄 {rel_path_unix}\n")
                try:
                    with open(os.path.join(root, file), "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        out.write(f.read())
                except:
                    out.write("[Unreadable file]")
                out.write("\n" + "-"*60 + "\n")
            else:
                out.write(f"  {file}\n")

print(f"✅ Clean snapshot created: {OUTPUT_FILE}")

## 📄 backend/app.py
import sys, io
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')
sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')

import os, shutil, uuid, time, threading, subprocess, queue, logging, mimetypes, sqlite3, bcrypt, secrets
from flask import Flask, request, send_file, render_template, Response, jsonify, make_response
from flask_cors import CORS, cross_origin
from pydub import AudioSegment
from pydub.utils import mediainfo
from pathlib import Path
from audio_splitter import split_audio
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from werkzeug.exceptions import RequestEntityTooLarge
from werkzeug.utils import secure_filename
from werkzeug.security import generate_password_hash
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from disposable_email_domains import blocklist
from datetime import datetime, timedelta


app = Flask(__name__)
CORS(app, resources={r"/*": {"origins": "*"}}, supports_credentials=True)

load_dotenv()
def get_env_int(name, default):
    try:
        return int(os.getenv(name, default))
    except:
        return default
    
MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH_MB = int(os.getenv("MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH", 100))
MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH = MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH_MB * 1024 * 1024
MAX_DISK_USAGE_MB = int(os.getenv("MAX_DISK_USAGE_MB", 1000))
MAX_JOBS_PER_IP = int(os.getenv("MAX_JOBS_PER_IP", 2))
MAX_UPLOADS_PER_MIN = int(os.getenv("MAX_UPLOADS_PER_MIN", 5))
TEMP_DIR = os.getenv("TEMP_DIR", "temp")
LOG_FILE = os.getenv("LOG_FILE", "logs/app.log")
JOB_EXPIRATION = int(os.getenv("JOB_EXPIRATION", 300))  # fallback 5 min



ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {".mp3", ".wav", ".flac", ".m4a", ".aac", ".ogg", ".aiff", ".mp4"}

app.config["MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH"] = MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH_MB * 1024 * 1024
os.makedirs(TEMP_DIR, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(LOG_FILE), exist_ok=True)

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s]: %(message)s",
                    handlers=[logging.FileHandler(LOG_FILE), logging.StreamHandler()])
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

JOBS = {}
UPLOAD_HISTORY = defaultdict(lambda: deque())
active_processing = set()
active_downloading = set()
jobs_lock = threading.Lock()

def get_db_connection():
    BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    db_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "db", "users.db")
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn

def allowed_file(filename):
    return filename and Path(filename).suffix.lower() in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS

def log(job_id, msg):
    logger.info(f"[{job_id}] {msg}")
    if job_id in JOBS:
        JOBS[job_id]["events"].put(msg)

def startup_cleanup():
    logger.info("Running startup cleanup of TEMP_DIR...")
    for item in os.listdir(TEMP_DIR):
        path = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, item)
        try:
            if os.path.isdir(path):
                shutil.rmtree(path, ignore_errors=True)
            else:
                os.remove(path)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Startup cleanup error on {path}: {e}")

def cleanup_old_temp():
    try:
        total_size = 0
        folder_stats = []
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(TEMP_DIR):
            for f in files:
                total_size += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, f))
            for d in dirs:
                folder_path = os.path.join(root, d)
                folder_size = sum(os.path.getsize(os.path.join(dp, file))
                                  for dp, dn, filenames in os.walk(folder_path) for file in filenames)
                last_modified = os.path.getmtime(folder_path)
                folder_stats.append((folder_path, folder_size, last_modified))

        if total_size / (1024 * 1024) > MAX_DISK_USAGE_MB:
            logger.warning("Disk usage exceeded, purging old temp folders...")
            for folder_path, _, _ in sorted(folder_stats, key=lambda x: (x[2], x[1])):
                folder_uuid = os.path.basename(folder_path)
                if folder_uuid in active_processing or folder_uuid in active_downloading:
                    continue
                shutil.rmtree(folder_path, ignore_errors=True)
                logger.info(f"Purged job folder: {folder_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Cleanup error: {e}")

def process_job(job_id, upload_path, filename, selected_stems):
    start_time = time.time()
    try:
        log(job_id, "Starting Demucs split...")

        demucs_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, "stems")
        preview_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, "preview")
        os.makedirs(preview_dir, exist_ok=True)
        base_name = os.path.splitext(filename)[0]
        zip_output_path = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, f"{base_name}_stems.zip")

        use_two_stems = sorted(selected_stems) == ["other", "vocals"]
        track_dir = split_audio(
            upload_path,
            output_dir=demucs_dir,
            stem_type="2stems" if use_two_stems else "all",
            job_id=job_id,
            logger=logger
        )

        all_possible_stems = {"vocals", "drums", "bass", "other", "no_vocals"}
        for stem in all_possible_stems:
            if stem == "no_vocals":
                if "other" in selected_stems:
                    continue
            elif stem not in selected_stems:
                file_path = os.path.join(track_dir, f"{stem}.wav")
                if os.path.exists(file_path):
                    os.remove(file_path)

        for stem in selected_stems:
            src = os.path.join(track_dir, f"{stem}.wav")
            if stem == "other" and not os.path.exists(src):
                fallback = os.path.join(track_dir, "no_vocals.wav")
                if os.path.exists(fallback):
                    src = fallback
            if os.path.exists(src):
                dst_preview = os.path.join(preview_dir, f"{stem}.mp3")
                audio = AudioSegment.from_wav(src)
                export_opts = {"format": "mp3", "bitrate": "192k"}
                if len(audio) > 30_000:
                    audio[:30_000].export(dst_preview, **export_opts)
                else:
                    audio.export(dst_preview, **export_opts)

        shutil.make_archive(zip_output_path.replace(".zip", ""), 'zip', track_dir)

        try:
            os.remove(upload_path)
        except Exception:
            pass
        shutil.rmtree(demucs_dir, ignore_errors=True)

        with jobs_lock:
            JOBS[job_id]["status"] = "done"
            JOBS[job_id]["zip"] = zip_output_path

        duration = time.time() - start_time
        log(job_id, f"Processing time: {duration:.2f} seconds")
        log(job_id, "Job complete. ZIP ready.")
        JOBS[job_id]["events"].put("Job complete")

        def auto_cleanup():
            time.sleep(JOB_EXPIRATION)
            with jobs_lock:
                if job_id in JOBS and JOBS[job_id]["status"] == "done":
                    folder = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id)
                    shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)
                    JOBS.pop(job_id, None)
                    logger.info(f"[CLEANUP] Auto-deleted job {job_id}")

        threading.Thread(target=auto_cleanup, daemon=True).start()

    except Exception as e:
        with jobs_lock:
            if job_id in JOBS:
                JOBS[job_id]["status"] = "error"
                JOBS[job_id]["zip"] = None
        log(job_id, f"Error: {e}")
    finally:
        active_processing.discard(job_id)



def is_rate_limited(ip):
    now = time.time()
    window = 60
    max_uploads = 2  # Your .env value

    with jobs_lock:
        history = UPLOAD_HISTORY[ip]

        # Remove expired timestamps
        while history and now - history[0] > window:
            history.popleft()

        # ✅ BLOCK if over frequency limit
        if len(history) >= max_uploads:
            return f"Upload rate limit exceeded ({max_uploads} per {window}s)."

        # ✅ Count this attempt no matter what
        history.append(now)

        # ✅ Then check active jobs
        active_jobs = sum(
            1 for job in JOBS.values()
            if job.get("ip") == ip and job.get("status") == "processing"
        )
        if active_jobs >= MAX_JOBS_PER_IP:
            return f"Too many jobs processing (limit: {MAX_JOBS_PER_IP})."

    return None


@app.route("/upload", methods=["POST"])
@cross_origin()
def upload():
    cleanup_old_temp()
    ip = request.remote_addr
    rate_limit_msg = is_rate_limited(ip)
    if rate_limit_msg:
        return jsonify({"error": rate_limit_msg}), 429

    if "file" not in request.files:
        return jsonify({"error": "No file uploaded."}), 400

    file = request.files["file"]
    filename = secure_filename(file.filename)
    if not filename or not allowed_file(filename):
        return jsonify({"error": "Unsupported file type."}), 400

    # 👇 Calculate file size
    file.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)
    file_size_mb = round(file.tell() / (1024 * 1024), 2)
    file.seek(0)

    # 👇 Check account status
    email = request.headers.get("X-User-Email")
    user_id = None
    is_guest = True
    db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")

    if email:
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
            c = conn.cursor()
            c.execute("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
            result = c.fetchone()
            if result:
                user_id = result[0]
                is_guest = False
        except Exception as e:
            print("Failed to look up user:", e)
        finally:
            if conn:
                conn.close()

    # 👇 Usage check
    if is_guest:
        weekly_usage = get_guest_weekly_usage(ip)
        cap = 150
    else:
        weekly_usage = get_account_weekly_usage(user_id)
        cap = 500

    if weekly_usage + file_size_mb > cap:
        return jsonify({
            "error": f"Weekly limit exceeded ({cap}MB max). You’ve used {round(weekly_usage, 2)}MB."
        }), 403

    selected_stems = request.form.getlist("stems") or ["vocals", "other"]
    job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    active_processing.add(job_id)

    temp_upload_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id)
    os.makedirs(temp_upload_dir, exist_ok=True)
    upload_path = os.path.join(temp_upload_dir, filename)
    file.save(upload_path)

    try:
        duration = float(mediainfo(upload_path).get("duration", "0"))
        if duration <= 0:
            raise ValueError("No valid audio stream")
        if duration > 600:
            raise ValueError("File too long")
    except Exception:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_upload_dir, ignore_errors=True)
        active_processing.discard(job_id)
        return jsonify({"error": "Invalid or corrupted audio file."}), 400

    if Path(filename).suffix.lower() == ".mp4":
        wav_path = os.path.join(temp_upload_dir, f"{Path(filename).stem}.wav")
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-i", upload_path, "-vn", "-acodec", "pcm_s16le", wav_path, "-y"]
        log(job_id, f"Extracting audio from MP4: {' '.join(cmd)}")
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
        if result.returncode != 0 or not os.path.exists(wav_path) or os.path.getsize(wav_path) < 4096:
            shutil.rmtree(temp_upload_dir, ignore_errors=True)
            active_processing.discard(job_id)
            return jsonify({"error": "Invalid or corrupted audio file."}), 400
        upload_path = wav_path
        log(job_id, "Extracted audio from MP4.")

    # 👇 Log usage to DB
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()
        upload_date = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO usage (user_id, ip, date, mb_used) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (user_id, ip, upload_date, file_size_mb)
        )
        conn.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        print("Failed to log usage:", e)
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()

    with jobs_lock:
        JOBS[job_id] = {"status": "processing", "zip": None, "events": queue.Queue(), "ip": ip}

    threading.Thread(target=process_job, args=(job_id, upload_path, filename, selected_stems)).start()
    return jsonify({"job_id": job_id})



@app.route("/stream/<job_id>")
@cross_origin()
def stream(job_id):
    def event_stream():
        q = JOBS.get(job_id, {}).get("events")
        if not q:
            yield "data: Job not found\n\n"
            return
        yield "data: Processing started...\n\n"
        while True:
            msg = q.get()
            yield f"data: {msg}\n\n"
            if "Job complete" in msg or "Error" in msg:
                break
    return Response(event_stream(), mimetype="text/event-stream")

@app.route("/download/<job_id>")
@cross_origin()
def download(job_id):
    job = JOBS.get(job_id)
    zip_path = job["zip"] if job else None

    if not job or not zip_path or not os.path.exists(zip_path):
        return jsonify({"error": "Download link expired. Please re-upload your file."}), 410

    temp_zip_path = zip_path + ".tmp"
    shutil.move(zip_path, temp_zip_path)
    folder_to_delete = os.path.dirname(temp_zip_path)
    active_downloading.add(job_id)

    with jobs_lock:
        JOBS.pop(job_id, None)

    filename = os.path.basename(zip_path)
    response = make_response(send_file(temp_zip_path, as_attachment=True))
    response.headers["Content-Type"] = mimetypes.guess_type(temp_zip_path)[0] or "application/octet-stream"
    response.headers["Content-Disposition"] = f'attachment; filename="{filename}"'
    response.headers["Access-Control-Expose-Headers"] = "Content-Disposition"

    def cleanup_job():
        time.sleep(2)
        active_downloading.discard(job_id)
        shutil.rmtree(folder_to_delete, ignore_errors=True)
        logger.info(f"Cleaned up temp folder: {folder_to_delete}")

    threading.Thread(target=cleanup_job).start()
    return response


@app.route("/preview/<job_id>/<stem>")
@cross_origin()
def preview(job_id, stem):
    preview_dir = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, job_id, "preview")
    if not os.path.exists(preview_dir):
        return jsonify({"error": "Preview not found or expired."}), 404

    path = os.path.join(preview_dir, f"{stem}.mp3")
    if stem == "other" and not os.path.exists(path):
        fallback = os.path.join(preview_dir, "no_vocals.wav")
        if os.path.exists(fallback):
            path = fallback

    if os.path.exists(path):
        return send_file(path, mimetype="audio/mpeg")
    return jsonify({"error": "Stem not found."}), 404


# SIGNUP
@app.route("/signup", methods=["POST"])
def signup():
    data = request.get_json()
    username = data.get("username")
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    if not username or not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"error": "All fields are required."}), 400

    # Check for disposable email
    domain = email.split("@")[-1]
    if domain in blocklist:
        return jsonify({"error": "Disposable email addresses are not allowed."}), 400

    conn = get_db_connection()
    c = conn.cursor()

    # Check if IP has signed up in last 7 days
    ip = request.remote_addr
    #c.execute('''
    #    SELECT * FROM signup_attempts
    #    WHERE ip_address = ? AND attempted_at >= datetime('now', '-7 days')
    #''', (ip,))
    #if c.fetchone():
    #    conn.close()
    #    return jsonify({"error": "Too many signups from this IP. Try again in 7 days."}), 429

    # Check if email or username already exists
    c.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ? OR username = ?", (email, username))
    if c.fetchone():
        conn.close()
        return jsonify({"error": "Email or username already in use."}), 409

    # Create user with is_verified = 0
    hashed_pw = generate_password_hash(password)
    c.execute("INSERT INTO users (username, email, password, is_verified) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)",
              (username, email, hashed_pw))
    user_id = c.lastrowid

    # Insert into signup_attempts
    c.execute("INSERT INTO signup_attempts (ip_address, attempted_at) VALUES (?, datetime('now'))", (ip,))

    # Generate token
    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    expires_at = (datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=24)).isoformat()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO verification_tokens (user_id, token, expires_at, used)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)
    ''', (user_id, token, expires_at))

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

    # Normally you'd send email here
    print(f"DEBUG: Email verification link → http://localhost:3000/verify-email/{token}")

    return jsonify({
    "user": username,
    "verified": False
    }), 200


# LOGIN
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
@cross_origin()
def login():
    data = request.json
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"error": "Email and password required."}), 400

    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()
        c.execute("SELECT password FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
        result = c.fetchone()
        conn.close()

        if not result:
            return jsonify({"error": "Invalid credentials."}), 401

        stored_pw = result[0]
        if not bcrypt.checkpw(password.encode("utf-8"), stored_pw.encode("utf-8")):
            return jsonify({"error": "Invalid credentials."}), 401

        return jsonify({"success": True}), 200

    except Exception as e:
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        return jsonify({"error": f"Server error: {str(e)}"}), 500
    
def get_guest_weekly_usage(ip):
    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()

        start_of_week = datetime.utcnow().date() - timedelta(days=datetime.utcnow().weekday())
        start_of_week_str = start_of_week.isoformat()

        c.execute("""
            SELECT SUM(mb_used) FROM usage
            WHERE ip = ? AND user_id IS NULL AND date >= ?
        """, (ip, start_of_week_str))

        result = c.fetchone()[0]
        return result or 0
    except Exception as e:
        print("Failed to fetch guest usage:", e)
        return 0
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()

@app.route("/usage", methods=["GET"])
@cross_origin()
def usage():
    ip = request.remote_addr
    email = request.headers.get("X-User-Email")
    is_guest = True
    user_id = None
    limit = 150
    used = 0

    try:
        db_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "db", "users.db")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        c = conn.cursor()

        start_of_week = datetime.utcnow().date() - timedelta(days=datetime.utcnow().weekday())
        start_of_week_str = start_of_week.isoformat()

        if email:
            c.execute("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?", (email,))
            result = c.fetchone()
            if result:
                user_id = result[0]
                is_guest = False

        if is_guest:
            c.execute("""
                SELECT SUM(mb_used) FROM usage
                WHERE ip = ? AND user_id IS NULL AND date >= ?
            """, (ip, start_of_week_str))
            limit = 150
        else:
            c.execute("""
                SELECT SUM(mb_used) FROM usage
                WHERE user_id = ? AND date >= ?
            """, (user_id, start_of_week_str))
            limit = 500  # Future: check is_premium flag

        used = c.fetchone()[0] or 0

        return jsonify({
            "used_mb": round(used, 2),
            "limit_mb": limit
        })

    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"error": "Failed to fetch usage"}), 500
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()

@app.route("/", defaults={"path": ""})
@app.route("/<path:path>")
def serve_react(path):
    from flask import send_from_directory
    if path != "" and os.path.exists(f"../frontend/dist/{path}"):
        return send_from_directory("../frontend/dist", path)
    else:
        return send_from_directory("../frontend/dist", "index.html")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run(debug=True, threaded=True)

## 📄 backend/audio_splitter.py

"""
audio_splitter.py
-----------------
Handles audio separation using Demucs.
Runs Demucs completely silent except for start and completion logs.
"""

import subprocess
import os

def split_audio(input_path, output_dir="separated", stem_type="all", job_id=None, logger=None, model_name="htdemucs"):
    """
    Split an audio file into stems using Demucs silently.

    Args:
        input_path (str): Path to the audio file to split.
        output_dir (str): Directory where separated stems are saved.
        stem_type (str): Type of separation ('all' or '2stems').
        job_id (str): Optional job ID for log prefixing.
        logger (logging.Logger): Optional Flask logger.

    Returns:
        str: Path to the directory containing separated stems.
    """
def split_audio(input_path, output_dir="separated", stem_type="all", job_id=None, logger=None):
    """
    Split an audio file into stems using Demucs silently.

    Args:
        input_path (str): Path to the audio file to split.
        output_dir (str): Directory where separated stems are saved.
        stem_type (str): Type of separation ('all' or '2stems').
        job_id (str): Optional job ID for log prefixing.
        logger (logging.Logger): Optional Flask logger.

    Returns:
        str: Path to the directory containing separated stems.
    """
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    model_args = []

    if stem_type == "2stems":
        model_args = ["--two-stems", "vocals"]

    command = ["demucs", input_path, "-o", output_dir] + model_args

    if logger:
        logger.info(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process starting...")
    else:
        print(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process starting...")

    subprocess.run(command, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)

    if logger:
        logger.info(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process complete.")
    else:
        print(f"[{job_id}] Demucs process complete.")

    base_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(input_path))[0]
    model_output_dir = os.listdir(output_dir)[0]
    output_path = os.path.join(output_dir, model_output_dir, base_name)

    if logger:
        logger.info(f"[{job_id}] Demucs wrote to: {os.path.join(output_dir, model_output_dir)}")

    return output_path

# 📁 backend\db

## 📄 backend/db/init_db.py
import sqlite3
import os

BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
db_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "users.db")

conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
c = conn.cursor()

# Users table
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        password TEXT NOT NULL,
        is_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0
    )
''')

# Usage table
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS usage (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        user_id INTEGER,
        ip TEXT,
        date TEXT,
        mb_used INTEGER
    )
''')

# verification token table 
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS verification_tokens (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        token TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        expires_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
        used BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,
        FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
    )
''')

# signup attemps table 
c.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS signup_attempts (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        ip_address TEXT NOT NULL,
        attempted_at DATETIME NOT NULL
    )
''')


conn.commit()
conn.close()

## 📄 frontend/src/api.js
import axios from "axios";

const API_BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:5000"; // Flask dev server URL

export async function uploadFile(file, selectedStems, onProgress, userEmail) {
  try {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append("file", file);
    selectedStems.forEach(stem => formData.append("stems", stem));

    const res = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/upload`, formData, {
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data",
        ...(userEmail && { "X-User-Email": userEmail })
      },
      onUploadProgress: (e) => {
        if (onProgress && e.total) {
          const percent = Math.round((e.loaded * 100) / e.total);
          onProgress(percent);
        }
      }
    });

    return res.data;
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error(err.response?.data?.error || "Upload failed.");
  }
}


export function streamJob(jobId, onMessage) {
  const evtSource = new EventSource(`${API_BASE}/stream/${jobId}`);
  evtSource.onmessage = (e) => {
    onMessage(e.data);
    if (e.data.includes("Job complete") || e.data.includes("Error")) {
      evtSource.close(); // ✅ Stop the stream after finishing
    }
  };
  return evtSource;
}

export async function downloadZip(jobId, onError) {
  try {
    const res = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/download/${jobId}`, {
      responseType: "blob"
    });

    let filename = "download.zip";
    const header = res.headers["content-disposition"];
    if (header && header.includes("filename=")) {
      filename = header.split("filename=")[1].replace(/"/g, "");
    }

    const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([res.data]));
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.setAttribute("download", filename);
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

  } catch (err) {
    if (onError) onError("Download failed. Please try again.");
  }
}

## 📄 frontend/src/components/Login.jsx
import React, { useState, useContext } from "react";
import axios from "axios";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function Login() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    const { login } = useContext(AuthContext);
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const isValidEmail = (email) => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError("");
        setSuccess(false);

        if (!email || !password) {
            setError("All fields are required.");
            return;
        }

        if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
            setError("Invalid email format.");
            return;
        }

        if (password.length < 6) {
            setError("Password must be at least 6 characters.");
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const res = await axios.post("http://127.0.0.1:5000/login", { email, password });

            if (res.data.success) {
                login(email);
                setSuccess(true);
                setEmail("");
                setPassword("");
                navigate("/dashboard"); // ✅ Auto redirect
            }
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Login failed.");
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-white px-4">
            <form
                onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                className="bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-lg w-full max-w-md space-y-4 shadow-lg"
            >
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center">Login</h2>

                {error && <div className="bg-red-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{error}</div>}
                {success && <div className="bg-green-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">Logged in successfully! ✅</div>}

                <input
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="Email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    className={`w-full bg-indigo-600 p-2 rounded font-semibold transition ${loading ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "hover:bg-indigo-700"}`}
                >
                    {loading ? (
                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-6 space-x-1">
                            {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
                                <div
                                    key={i}
                                    className="w-1 bg-white rounded animate-pulse"
                                    style={{
                                        animationDelay: `${i * 0.15}s`,
                                        animationDuration: "1s",
                                        height: "100%",
                                        transformOrigin: "center bottom",
                                        animationName: "wavePulse"
                                    }}
                                />
                            ))}
                            <style>
                                {`
                                  @keyframes wavePulse {
                                    0%, 100% { transform: scaleY(0.3); opacity: 0.5; }
                                    50% { transform: scaleY(1); opacity: 1; }
                                  }
                                  .animate-pulse {
                                    animation: wavePulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
                                  }
                                `}
                            </style>
                        </div>
                    ) : "Login"}
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}

import React, { useContext, useState } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function Navbar() {
  const { user, logout } = useContext(AuthContext);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  return (
    <>
      <header className="fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-50 bg-transparent">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 py-4 grid grid-cols-3 items-center">
          {/* Logo (left) */}
          <Link to="/" className="text-white text-2xl font-bold">
            EchoMind
          </Link>

          {/* Nav links (centered) */}
          <nav className="hidden md:flex justify-center space-x-6 text-white">
            <Link to="/" className="hover:text-gray-300">Home</Link>
            <Link to="/faq" className="hover:text-gray-300">FAQ</Link>
            {user && <Link to="/dashboard" className="hover:text-gray-300">Dashboard</Link>}
          </nav>

          {/* Auth buttons (right) */}
          <div className="hidden md:flex justify-end space-x-4">
            {!user ? (
              <>
                <Link to="/login" className="text-white hover:text-gray-300">Login</Link>
                <Link to="/signup" className="text-white hover:text-gray-300">Sign Up</Link>
              </>
            ) : (
              <button onClick={handleLogout} className="text-white hover:text-red-400">
                Logout
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Hamburger (right) */}
          <div className="flex justify-end md:hidden">
            <button className="focus:outline-none" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" className="w-8 h-8 text-white" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16" />
              </svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Mobile overlay */}
      {open && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-40" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} />
      )}

      {/* Slide-out menu */}
      <div className={`fixed top-0 right-0 h-full w-2/3 max-w-xs bg-gray-900 z-50 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out ${open ? "translate-x-0" : "translate-x-full"}`}>
        <div className="p-6 flex flex-col space-y-4 text-white">
          <button className="self-end text-2xl font-bold focus:outline-none" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>✕</button>
          <Link to="/" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Home</Link>
          <Link to="/faq" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>FAQ</Link>
          {user && (
            <Link to="/dashboard" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Dashboard</Link>
          )}
          {!user ? (
            <>
              <Link to="/login" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Login</Link>
              <Link to="/signup" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Sign Up</Link>
            </>
          ) : (
            <button onClick={() => { setOpen(false); handleLogout(); }} className="text-left text-lg hover:text-red-400">Logout</button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

------------------------------------------------------------

## 📄 frontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx
import React, { useContext } from "react";
import { Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";

export default function ProtectedRoute({ children }) {
    const { user } = useContext(AuthContext);

    if (!user) {
        return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
    }

    return children;
}
