## 📄 frontend/src/components/Signup.jsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function Signup() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
    const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const isValidEmail = (email) => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError("");
        setSuccess(false);

        if (!email || !username || !password) {
            setError("All fields are required.");
            return;
        }

        if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
            setError("Invalid email format.");
            return;
        }

        if (password.length < 6) {
            setError("Password must be at least 6 characters.");
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const res = await axios.post("http://127.0.0.1:5000/signup", {
                email,
                username,
                password
            });

            if (res.status === 200 && res.data?.user) {
                setSuccess(true);
                setEmail("");
                setUsername("");
                setPassword("");

                // 🔁 Optional: redirect after a short delay
                setTimeout(() => {
                    navigate("/login");
                }, 1500);
            } else {
                setError("Unexpected response from server.");
            }

        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Signup failed.");
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-white px-4">
            <form
                onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                className="bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-lg w-full max-w-md space-y-4 shadow-lg"
            >
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center">Sign Up</h2>

                {error && <div className="bg-red-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{error}</div>}
                {success && <div className="bg-green-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">Signup successful! 🎉</div>}

                <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Username"
                    value={username}
                    onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <input
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="Email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    className={`w-full bg-indigo-600 p-2 rounded font-semibold transition ${loading ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "hover:bg-indigo-700"}`}
                >
                    {loading ? (
                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-6 space-x-1">
                            {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
                                <div
                                    key={i}
                                    className="w-1 bg-white rounded animate-pulse"
                                    style={{
                                        animationDelay: `${i * 0.15}s`,
                                        animationDuration: "1s",
                                        height: "100%",
                                        transformOrigin: "center bottom",
                                        animationName: "wavePulse"
                                    }}
                                />
                            ))}
                            <style>
                                {`
                                  @keyframes wavePulse {
                                    0%, 100% { transform: scaleY(0.3); opacity: 0.5; }
                                    50% { transform: scaleY(1); opacity: 1; }
                                  }
                                  .animate-pulse {
                                    animation: wavePulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
                                  }
                                `}
                            </style>
                        </div>
                    ) : "Sign Up"}
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}

------------------------------------------------------------

## 📄 frontend/src/components/UploadForm.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import WaveSurfer from "wavesurfer.js";
import { uploadFile, streamJob, downloadZip } from "../api";
import axios from "axios";
import { useContext } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";

export default function UploadForm() {
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [stems, setStems] = useState(["vocals", "other"]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [jobId, setJobId] = useState(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [showPreview, setShowPreview] = useState(true);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [evtSource, setEvtSource] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const waveRefs = useRef({});
  const [playingStates, setPlayingStates] = useState({});
  const [volumes, setVolumes] = useState({});
  const { user } = useContext(AuthContext);

  // ✅ Usage state
  const [usedMB, setUsedMB] = useState(0);
  const [limitMB, setLimitMB] = useState(150);

  const fetchUsage = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await axios.get("http://127.0.0.1:5000/usage", {
        headers: {
          ...(user?.email && { "X-User-Email": user.email })
        }
      });
      setUsedMB(res.data.used_mb);
      setLimitMB(res.data.limit_mb);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to fetch usage", err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchUsage();
  }, []);

  const toggleStem = (stem) => {
    setStems((prev) =>
      prev.includes(stem) ? prev.filter((s) => s !== stem) : [...prev, stem]
    );
  };

  const startUpload = async (selectedFile) => {
    if (!selectedFile) return;
    setFile(selectedFile);
    setUploading(true);
    setStatus("Uploading...");
    setDone(false);
    setShowPreview(true);
    setError("");

    console.log("Logged in as:", user?.email); 

    let res;
    try {
      res = await uploadFile(selectedFile, stems, null, user?.email);
      if (!res?.job_id) throw new Error("No job ID received.");
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setError(err.message || "Upload failed. Please try again.");
      setUploading(false);
      return;
    }

    setJobId(res.job_id);
    setStatus("Processing...");

    const es = streamJob(res.job_id, (msg) => {
      setStatus(msg);
      if (msg.includes("Job complete")) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          setDone(true);
          setUploading(false);
          fetchUsage(); // ✅ update usage after job finishes
        }, 500);
      }
    });

    setEvtSource(es);
  };

  const handleDownload = () => {
    if (jobId) {
      downloadZip(jobId, (errMsg) => setError(errMsg));
      setFile(null);
      setJobId(null);
      setDone(false);
      setStatus("");
      setShowPreview(false);
      setStems(["vocals", "other"]);
      setUploading(false);
      setPlayingStates({});
      setVolumes({});
    }
  };

  const handleFileSelect = (e) => {
    console.log("FILE SELECTED");
    const selectedFile = e.target.files[0];
    if (selectedFile) startUpload(selectedFile);
  };

  const handleDrop = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const droppedFile = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
    if (droppedFile) startUpload(droppedFile);
  };

  const handleDragOver = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (evtSource) evtSource.close();
      Object.values(waveRefs.current).forEach((ws) => ws.destroy());
    };
  }, [evtSource]);

  const createWaveform = (url, stem) => {
    const container = document.getElementById(`waveform-${stem}`);
    if (!container) return;

    if (waveRefs.current[stem]) {
      waveRefs.current[stem].destroy();
    }

    waveRefs.current[stem] = WaveSurfer.create({
      container: container,
      waveColor: "#4F46E5",
      progressColor: "#22C55E",
      cursorColor: "#FFFFFF",
      height: 80,
      barWidth: 2,
      responsive: true,
      volume: volumes[stem] ?? 1,
    });

    waveRefs.current[stem].load(url);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (done && showPreview && jobId) {
      stems.forEach((stem) => {
        const url = `http://127.0.0.1:5000/preview/${jobId}/${stem}`;
        createWaveform(url, stem);
      });
    }
  }, [done, showPreview, jobId]);

  const toggleStemPlay = (stem) => {
    const ws = waveRefs.current[stem];
    if (!ws) return;
    if (playingStates[stem]) {
      ws.pause();
      setPlayingStates((prev) => ({ ...prev, [stem]: false }));
    } else {
      ws.play();
      setPlayingStates((prev) => ({ ...prev, [stem]: true }));
    }
  };

  const handleVolumeChange = (stem, value) => {
    const vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
    setVolumes((prev) => ({ ...prev, [stem]: vol }));
    if (waveRefs.current[stem]) {
      waveRefs.current[stem].setVolume(vol);
    }
  };

  const startDrag = (stem, e) => {
    const bar = e.currentTarget;

    const updateVolume = (event) => {
      const rect = bar.getBoundingClientRect();
      const y = event.clientY - rect.top;
      const height = rect.height;
      const newVol = 1 - y / height;
      handleVolumeChange(stem, newVol);
    };

    updateVolume(e);

    const moveHandler = (event) => updateVolume(event);
    const upHandler = () => {
      window.removeEventListener("mousemove", moveHandler);
      window.removeEventListener("mouseup", upHandler);
    };

    window.addEventListener("mousemove", moveHandler);
    window.addEventListener("mouseup", upHandler);
  };

  const usagePercent = Math.min(100, Math.round((usedMB / limitMB) * 100));

  return (
    <div
      className="bg-gray-800 bg-opacity-50 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg"
      onDrop={handleDrop}
      onDragOver={handleDragOver}
    >
      {/* ✅ USAGE BAR */}
      <div className="mb-4">
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-200 mb-1">
          📊 Usage: {usedMB}MB / {limitMB}MB ({usagePercent}%)
        </p>
        <div className="w-full h-3 bg-gray-600 rounded">
          <div
            className={`h-3 rounded transition-all duration-300 ${
              usagePercent < 60
                ? "bg-green-500"
                : usagePercent < 90
                ? "bg-yellow-400"
                : "bg-red-500"
            }`}
            style={{ width: `${usagePercent}%` }}
          ></div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ERROR */}
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-600 bg-opacity-80 rounded text-white text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* UPLOAD + STEMS */}
      {!done && !uploading && (
        <>
          <label className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-full h-32 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-500 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:border-indigo-400 transition">
            <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm mb-2">
              Drag & Drop or Click to Select a File
            </p>
            <input
              type="file"
              onChange={handleFileSelect}
              className="hidden"
            />
          </label>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 justify-center mt-4">
            {["vocals", "drums", "bass", "other"].map((stem) => (
              <label key={stem} className="text-sm">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  className="mr-2"
                  checked={stems.includes(stem)}
                  onChange={() => toggleStem(stem)}
                  disabled={uploading || jobId !== null}
                />
                {stem.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + stem.slice(1)}
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {/* LOADING */}
      {uploading && !done && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center mt-8">
          <div className="flex space-x-1 h-8">
            {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="w-2 bg-indigo-500 rounded animate-pulse"
                style={{
                  animationDelay: `${i * 0.15}s`,
                  animationDuration: "1s",
                  height: "100%",
                  transformOrigin: "center bottom",
                  animationName: "wavePulse",
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="mt-4 text-gray-300 text-sm">{status}</p>

          <style>
            {`
              @keyframes wavePulse {
                0%, 100% { transform: scaleY(0.3); opacity: 0.5; }
                50% { transform: scaleY(1); opacity: 1; }
              }
              .animate-pulse {
                animation: wavePulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
              }
            `}
          </style>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* DOWNLOAD + PREVIEW */}
      {done && (
        <div className="mt-6">
          <button
            onClick={handleDownload}
            className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium"
          >
            Download Stems
          </button>

          {showPreview && (
            <div className="mt-6 bg-gray-900 p-4 rounded-lg">
              <h3 className="text-white mb-2">Preview Stems</h3>
              {stems.map((stem) => (
                <div key={stem} className="mb-6 flex items-center space-x-3">
                  <div className="min-w-[60px] text-sm text-white font-medium capitalize">
                    {stem}
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => toggleStemPlay(stem)}
                    className="bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 p-2 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"
                  >
                    <svg
                      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                      className="w-6 h-6 text-white"
                      fill="currentColor"
                      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                    >
                      {playingStates[stem] ? (
                        <path d="M6 4h4v16H6zm8 0h4v16h-4z" />
                      ) : (
                        <path d="M8 5v14l11-7z" />
                      )}
                    </svg>
                  </button>

                  <div
                    id={`waveform-${stem}`}
                    className="flex-1 bg-black rounded"
                  ></div>

                  <div
                    className="relative w-8 h-32 bg-gray-700 rounded-lg cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-end items-center overflow-hidden"
                    onMouseDown={(e) => startDrag(stem, e)}
                  >
                    <div
                      className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"
                      style={{
                        height: `${(volumes[stem] ?? 1) * 100}%`,
                        background: "linear-gradient(to top, #22C55E, #4F46E5)",
                      }}
                    ></div>
                    <span className="relative text-xs text-white font-bold mb-1 z-10">
                      {Math.round((volumes[stem] ?? 1) * 100)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

# 📁 frontend\src\context

## 📄 frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx
import React from "react";
import { createContext, useState, useEffect } from "react";

export const AuthContext = createContext();

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
    const [user, setUser] = useState(() => {
        try {
            const stored = localStorage.getItem("user");
            return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : null;
        } catch {
            return null;
        }
    });

    const login = (email) => {
        const newUser = { email };
        setUser(newUser);
        localStorage.setItem("user", JSON.stringify(newUser));
    };

    const logout = () => {
        setUser(null);
        localStorage.removeItem("user");
    };

    return (
        <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, login, logout }}>
            {children}
        </AuthContext.Provider>
    );
}

=== frontend\src\api.js ===

import axios from "axios";

const API_BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:5000"; // Flask dev server URL

export async function uploadFile(file, selectedStems, onProgress, userEmail) {
  try {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append("file", file);
    selectedStems.forEach(stem => formData.append("stems", stem));

    const res = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/upload`, formData, {
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data",
        ...(userEmail && { "X-User-Email": userEmail })
      },
      onUploadProgress: (e) => {
        if (onProgress && e.total) {
          const percent = Math.round((e.loaded * 100) / e.total);
          onProgress(percent);
        }
      }
    });

    return res.data;
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error(err.response?.data?.error || "Upload failed.");
  }
}


export function streamJob(jobId, onMessage) {
  const evtSource = new EventSource(`${API_BASE}/stream/${jobId}`);
  evtSource.onmessage = (e) => {
    onMessage(e.data);
    if (e.data.includes("Job complete") || e.data.includes("Error")) {
      evtSource.close(); // ✅ Stop the stream after finishing
    }
  };
  return evtSource;
}

export async function downloadZip(jobId, onError) {
  try {
    const res = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/download/${jobId}`, {
      responseType: "blob"
    });

    let filename = "download.zip";
    const header = res.headers["content-disposition"];
    if (header && header.includes("filename=")) {
      filename = header.split("filename=")[1].replace(/"/g, "");
    }

    const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([res.data]));
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.setAttribute("download", filename);
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

  } catch (err) {
    if (onError) onError("Download failed. Please try again.");
  }
}

=== frontend\src\App.jsx ===

import React from "react";
import { Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import Navbar from "./components/Navbar";
import Hero from "./components/Hero";
import Faq from "./components/Faq";
import Signup from "./components/Signup";
import Login from "./components/Login";
import Dashboard from "./components/Dashboard";
import ProtectedRoute from "./components/ProtectedRoute";
import ResetPasswordRequest from "./components/ResetPasswordRequest";
import ResetPasswordConfirm from "./components/ResetPasswordConfirm";

export default function App() {
  return (
    <div className="bg-black min-h-screen">
      <Navbar />
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<Hero />} />
        <Route path="/faq" element={<Faq />} />
        <Route path="/signup" element={<Signup />} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
        <Route path="/reset-password" element={<ResetPasswordRequest />} />
        <Route path="/reset-password-confirm/:token" element={<ResetPasswordConfirm />} />
        <Route
          path="/dashboard"
          element={
            <ProtectedRoute>
              <Dashboard />
            </ProtectedRoute>
          }
        />
      </Routes>
    </div>
  );
}

=== frontend\src\components\Login.jsx ===

import React, { useState, useContext } from "react";
import axios from "axios";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function Login() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    const { login } = useContext(AuthContext);
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const isValidEmail = (email) => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError("");
        setSuccess(false);

        if (!email || !password) {
            setError("All fields are required.");
            return;
        }

        if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
            setError("Invalid email format.");
            return;
        }

        if (password.length < 6) {
            setError("Password must be at least 6 characters.");
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const res = await axios.post("http://127.0.0.1:5000/login", { email, password });

            if (res.data.success) {
                login(email);
                setSuccess(true);
                setEmail("");
                setPassword("");
                navigate("/dashboard"); // ✅ Auto redirect
            }
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Login failed.");
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-white px-4">
            <form
                onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                className="bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-lg w-full max-w-md space-y-4 shadow-lg"
            >
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center">Login</h2>

                {error && <div className="bg-red-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{error}</div>}
                {success && <div className="bg-green-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">Logged in successfully! ✅</div>}

                <input
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="Email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <div className="text-sm text-right">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigate("/reset-password")}
                        className="text-indigo-400 hover:underline"
                    >
                        Forgot password?
                    </button>
                </div>
                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    className={`w-full bg-indigo-600 p-2 rounded font-semibold transition ${loading ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "hover:bg-indigo-700"}`}
                >
                    {loading ? (
                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-6 space-x-1">
                            {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
                                <div
                                    key={i}
                                    className="w-1 bg-white rounded animate-pulse"
                                    style={{
                                        animationDelay: `${i * 0.15}s`,
                                        animationDuration: "1s",
                                        height: "100%",
                                        transformOrigin: "center bottom",
                                        animationName: "wavePulse"
                                    }}
                                />
                            ))}
                            <style>
                                {`
                                  @keyframes wavePulse {
                                    0%, 100% { transform: scaleY(0.3); opacity: 0.5; }
                                    50% { transform: scaleY(1); opacity: 1; }
                                  }
                                  .animate-pulse {
                                    animation: wavePulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
                                  }
                                `}
                            </style>
                        </div>
                    ) : "Login"}
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}

=== frontend\src\components\Navbar.jsx ===

import React, { useContext, useState } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function Navbar() {
  const { user, logout } = useContext(AuthContext);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  return (
    <>
      <header className="fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-50 bg-transparent">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 py-4 grid grid-cols-3 items-center">
          {/* Logo (left) */}
          <Link to="/" className="text-white text-2xl font-bold">
            EchoMind
          </Link>

          {/* Nav links (centered) */}
          <nav className="hidden md:flex justify-center space-x-6 text-white">
            <Link to="/" className="hover:text-gray-300">Home</Link>
            <Link to="/faq" className="hover:text-gray-300">FAQ</Link>
            {user && <Link to="/dashboard" className="hover:text-gray-300">Dashboard</Link>}
          </nav>

          {/* Auth buttons (right) */}
          <div className="hidden md:flex justify-end space-x-4">
            {!user ? (
              <>
                <Link to="/login" className="text-white hover:text-gray-300">Login</Link>
                <Link to="/signup" className="text-white hover:text-gray-300">Sign Up</Link>
              </>
            ) : (
              <button onClick={handleLogout} className="text-white hover:text-red-400">
                Logout
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Hamburger (right) */}
          <div className="flex justify-end md:hidden">
            <button className="focus:outline-none" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" className="w-8 h-8 text-white" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16" />
              </svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Mobile overlay */}
      {open && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-40" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} />
      )}

      {/* Slide-out menu */}
      <div className={`fixed top-0 right-0 h-full w-2/3 max-w-xs bg-gray-900 z-50 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out ${open ? "translate-x-0" : "translate-x-full"}`}>
        <div className="p-6 flex flex-col space-y-4 text-white">
          <button className="self-end text-2xl font-bold focus:outline-none" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>✕</button>
          <Link to="/" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Home</Link>
          <Link to="/faq" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>FAQ</Link>
          {user && (
            <Link to="/dashboard" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Dashboard</Link>
          )}
          {!user ? (
            <>
              <Link to="/login" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Login</Link>
              <Link to="/signup" className="text-lg hover:text-gray-300" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Sign Up</Link>
            </>
          ) : (
            <button onClick={() => { setOpen(false); handleLogout(); }} className="text-left text-lg hover:text-red-400">Logout</button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}


========================================

=== frontend\src\components\ProtectedRoute.jsx ===

import React, { useContext } from "react";
import { Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";

export default function ProtectedRoute({ children }) {
    const { user } = useContext(AuthContext);

    if (!user) {
        return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
    }

    return children;
}

=== frontend\src\components\ResetPasswordConfirm.jsx ===

import React, { useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";
import { useParams, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function ResetPasswordConfirm() {
    const { token } = useParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError("");
        setMessage("");

        if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
            setError("Passwords do not match.");
            return;
        }

        if (newPassword.length < 6) {
            setError("Password must be at least 6 characters.");
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const res = await axios.post(`http://127.0.0.1:5000/reset-password-confirm/${token}`, {
                new_password: newPassword
            });

            if (res.data.success) {
                setMessage("Password reset successfully!");
                setTimeout(() => navigate("/login"), 2000);
            } else {
                setError("Invalid or expired token.");
            }
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Reset failed.");
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-white px-4">
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-lg w-full max-w-md space-y-4 shadow-lg">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center">Set New Password</h2>

                {error && <div className="bg-red-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{error}</div>}
                {message && <div className="bg-green-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{message}</div>}

                <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="New Password"
                    value={newPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                    required
                />
                <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Confirm New Password"
                    value={confirmPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                    required
                />

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    className={`w-full bg-indigo-600 p-2 rounded font-semibold transition ${loading ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "hover:bg-indigo-700"
                        }`}
                >
                    {loading ? "Resetting..." : "Reset Password"}
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}

=== frontend\src\components\ResetPasswordRequest.jsx ===

import React, { useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";

export default function ResetPasswordRequest() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError("");
        setMessage("");
        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const res = await axios.post("http://127.0.0.1:5000/reset-password-request", { email });

            if (res.data.success) {
                setMessage("Password reset link sent to your email.");
                setEmail("");
            } else {
                setError("Something went wrong. Try again.");
            }
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Request failed.");
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-white px-4">
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-lg w-full max-w-md space-y-4 shadow-lg">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center">Reset Password</h2>

                {error && <div className="bg-red-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{error}</div>}
                {message && <div className="bg-green-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{message}</div>}

                <input
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="Enter your email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                    required
                />

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    className={`w-full bg-indigo-600 p-2 rounded font-semibold transition ${loading ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "hover:bg-indigo-700"
                        }`}
                >
                    {loading ? "Sending..." : "Send Reset Link"}
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}

=== frontend\src\components\Signup.jsx ===

import React, { useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function Signup() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
    const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const isValidEmail = (email) => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError("");
        setSuccess(false);

        if (!email || !username || !password) {
            setError("All fields are required.");
            return;
        }

        if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
            setError("Invalid email format.");
            return;
        }

        if (password.length < 6) {
            setError("Password must be at least 6 characters.");
            return;
        }

        if (password !== confirmPassword) {
            setError("Passwords do not match.");
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const res = await axios.post("http://127.0.0.1:5000/signup", {
                email,
                username,
                password
            });

            if (res.status === 200 && res.data?.user) {
                setSuccess(true);
                setEmail("");
                setUsername("");
                setPassword("");

                // 🔁 Optional: redirect after a short delay
                setTimeout(() => {
                    navigate("/login");
                }, 1500);
            } else {
                setError("Unexpected response from server.");
            }

        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Signup failed.");
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-white px-4">
            <form
                onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                className="bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-lg w-full max-w-md space-y-4 shadow-lg"
            >
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center">Sign Up</h2>

                {error && <div className="bg-red-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">{error}</div>}
                {success && <div className="bg-green-600 p-2 rounded text-sm">Signup successful! 🎉</div>}

                <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Username"
                    value={username}
                    onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <input
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="Email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />
                <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Confirm password"
                    value={confirmPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-900 rounded"
                />

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    className={`w-full bg-indigo-600 p-2 rounded font-semibold transition ${loading ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "hover:bg-indigo-700"}`}
                >
                    {loading ? (
                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-6 space-x-1">
                            {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
                                <div
                                    key={i}
                                    className="w-1 bg-white rounded animate-pulse"
                                    style={{
                                        animationDelay: `${i * 0.15}s`,
                                        animationDuration: "1s",
                                        height: "100%",
                                        transformOrigin: "center bottom",
                                        animationName: "wavePulse"
                                    }}
                                />
                            ))}
                            <style>
                                {`
                                  @keyframes wavePulse {
                                    0%, 100% { transform: scaleY(0.3); opacity: 0.5; }
                                    50% { transform: scaleY(1); opacity: 1; }
                                  }
                                  .animate-pulse {
                                    animation: wavePulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
                                  }
                                `}
                            </style>
                        </div>
                    ) : "Sign Up"}
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}

=== frontend\src\components\UploadForm.jsx ===

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import WaveSurfer from "wavesurfer.js";
import { uploadFile, streamJob, downloadZip } from "../api";
import axios from "axios";
import { useContext } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";

export default function UploadForm() {
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [stems, setStems] = useState(["vocals", "other"]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [jobId, setJobId] = useState(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [showPreview, setShowPreview] = useState(true);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [evtSource, setEvtSource] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const waveRefs = useRef({});
  const [playingStates, setPlayingStates] = useState({});
  const [volumes, setVolumes] = useState({});
  const { user } = useContext(AuthContext);

  // ✅ Usage state
  const [usedMB, setUsedMB] = useState(0);
  const [limitMB, setLimitMB] = useState(150);

  const fetchUsage = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await axios.get("http://127.0.0.1:5000/usage", {
        headers: {
          ...(user?.email && { "X-User-Email": user.email })
        }
      });
      setUsedMB(res.data.used_mb);
      setLimitMB(res.data.limit_mb);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to fetch usage", err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchUsage();
  }, []);

  const toggleStem = (stem) => {
    setStems((prev) =>
      prev.includes(stem) ? prev.filter((s) => s !== stem) : [...prev, stem]
    );
  };

  const startUpload = async (selectedFile) => {
    if (!selectedFile) return;
    setFile(selectedFile);
    setUploading(true);
    setStatus("Uploading...");
    setDone(false);
    setShowPreview(true);
    setError("");

    console.log("Logged in as:", user?.email); 

    let res;
    try {
      res = await uploadFile(selectedFile, stems, null, user?.email);
      if (!res?.job_id) throw new Error("No job ID received.");
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setError(err.message || "Upload failed. Please try again.");
      setUploading(false);
      return;
    }

    setJobId(res.job_id);
    setStatus("Processing...");

    const es = streamJob(res.job_id, (msg) => {
      setStatus(msg);
      if (msg.includes("Job complete")) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          setDone(true);
          setUploading(false);
          fetchUsage(); // ✅ update usage after job finishes
        }, 500);
      }
    });

    setEvtSource(es);
  };

  const handleDownload = () => {
    if (jobId) {
      downloadZip(jobId, (errMsg) => setError(errMsg));
      setFile(null);
      setJobId(null);
      setDone(false);
      setStatus("");
      setShowPreview(false);
      setStems(["vocals", "other"]);
      setUploading(false);
      setPlayingStates({});
      setVolumes({});
    }
  };

  const handleFileSelect = (e) => {
    console.log("FILE SELECTED");
    const selectedFile = e.target.files[0];
    if (selectedFile) startUpload(selectedFile);
  };

  const handleDrop = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const droppedFile = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
    if (droppedFile) startUpload(droppedFile);
  };

  const handleDragOver = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (evtSource) evtSource.close();
      Object.values(waveRefs.current).forEach((ws) => ws.destroy());
    };
  }, [evtSource]);

  const createWaveform = (url, stem) => {
    const container = document.getElementById(`waveform-${stem}`);
    if (!container) return;

    if (waveRefs.current[stem]) {
      waveRefs.current[stem].destroy();
    }

    waveRefs.current[stem] = WaveSurfer.create({
      container: container,
      waveColor: "#4F46E5",
      progressColor: "#22C55E",
      cursorColor: "#FFFFFF",
      height: 80,
      barWidth: 2,
      responsive: true,
      volume: volumes[stem] ?? 1,
    });

    waveRefs.current[stem].load(url);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (done && showPreview && jobId) {
      stems.forEach((stem) => {
        const url = `http://127.0.0.1:5000/preview/${jobId}/${stem}`;
        createWaveform(url, stem);
      });
    }
  }, [done, showPreview, jobId]);

  const toggleStemPlay = (stem) => {
    const ws = waveRefs.current[stem];
    if (!ws) return;
    if (playingStates[stem]) {
      ws.pause();
      setPlayingStates((prev) => ({ ...prev, [stem]: false }));
    } else {
      ws.play();
      setPlayingStates((prev) => ({ ...prev, [stem]: true }));
    }
  };

  const handleVolumeChange = (stem, value) => {
    const vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
    setVolumes((prev) => ({ ...prev, [stem]: vol }));
    if (waveRefs.current[stem]) {
      waveRefs.current[stem].setVolume(vol);
    }
  };

  const startDrag = (stem, e) => {
    const bar = e.currentTarget;

    const updateVolume = (event) => {
      const rect = bar.getBoundingClientRect();
      const y = event.clientY - rect.top;
      const height = rect.height;
      const newVol = 1 - y / height;
      handleVolumeChange(stem, newVol);
    };

    updateVolume(e);

    const moveHandler = (event) => updateVolume(event);
    const upHandler = () => {
      window.removeEventListener("mousemove", moveHandler);
      window.removeEventListener("mouseup", upHandler);
    };

    window.addEventListener("mousemove", moveHandler);
    window.addEventListener("mouseup", upHandler);
  };

  const usagePercent = Math.min(100, Math.round((usedMB / limitMB) * 100));

  return (
    <div
      className="bg-gray-800 bg-opacity-50 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg"
      onDrop={handleDrop}
      onDragOver={handleDragOver}
    >
      {/* ✅ USAGE BAR */}
      <div className="mb-4">
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-200 mb-1">
          📊 Usage: {usedMB}MB / {limitMB}MB ({usagePercent}%)
        </p>
        <div className="w-full h-3 bg-gray-600 rounded">
          <div
            className={`h-3 rounded transition-all duration-300 ${
              usagePercent < 60
                ? "bg-green-500"
                : usagePercent < 90
                ? "bg-yellow-400"
                : "bg-red-500"
            }`}
            style={{ width: `${usagePercent}%` }}
          ></div>
        </div>
        {/* ⛔ Near-limit warning */}
        {usagePercent >= 85 && (
          <p className="text-sm text-red-500 font-medium mt-1">
            ⚠️ You’re close to your weekly limit!
          </p>
        )}

        {/* 🕒 Reset info */}
        <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
          Usage resets every Monday at 12:00 AM (AEST)
        </p>
      </div>
      
      
      {/* ERROR */}
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-600 bg-opacity-80 rounded text-white text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* UPLOAD + STEMS */}
      {!done && !uploading && (
        <>
          <label className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-full h-32 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-500 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:border-indigo-400 transition">
            <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm mb-2">
              Drag & Drop or Click to Select a File
            </p>
            <input
              type="file"
              onChange={handleFileSelect}
              className="hidden"
            />
          </label>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 justify-center mt-4">
            {["vocals", "drums", "bass", "other"].map((stem) => (
              <label key={stem} className="text-sm">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  className="mr-2"
                  checked={stems.includes(stem)}
                  onChange={() => toggleStem(stem)}
                  disabled={uploading || jobId !== null}
                />
                {stem.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + stem.slice(1)}
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {/* LOADING */}
      {uploading && !done && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center mt-8">
          <div className="flex space-x-1 h-8">
            {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="w-2 bg-indigo-500 rounded animate-pulse"
                style={{
                  animationDelay: `${i * 0.15}s`,
                  animationDuration: "1s",
                  height: "100%",
                  transformOrigin: "center bottom",
                  animationName: "wavePulse",
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="mt-4 text-gray-300 text-sm">{status}</p>

          <style>
            {`
              @keyframes wavePulse {
                0%, 100% { transform: scaleY(0.3); opacity: 0.5; }
                50% { transform: scaleY(1); opacity: 1; }
              }
              .animate-pulse {
                animation: wavePulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
              }
            `}
          </style>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* DOWNLOAD + PREVIEW */}
      {done && (
        <div className="mt-6">
          <button
            onClick={handleDownload}
            className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium"
          >
            Download Stems
          </button>

          {showPreview && (
            <div className="mt-6 bg-gray-900 p-4 rounded-lg">
              <h3 className="text-white mb-2">Preview Stems</h3>
              {stems.map((stem) => (
                <div key={stem} className="mb-6 flex items-center space-x-3">
                  <div className="min-w-[60px] text-sm text-white font-medium capitalize">
                    {stem}
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => toggleStemPlay(stem)}
                    className="bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 p-2 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"
                  >
                    <svg
                      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                      className="w-6 h-6 text-white"
                      fill="currentColor"
                      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                    >
                      {playingStates[stem] ? (
                        <path d="M6 4h4v16H6zm8 0h4v16h-4z" />
                      ) : (
                        <path d="M8 5v14l11-7z" />
                      )}
                    </svg>
                  </button>

                  <div
                    id={`waveform-${stem}`}
                    className="flex-1 bg-black rounded"
                  ></div>

                  <div
                    className="relative w-8 h-32 bg-gray-700 rounded-lg cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-end items-center overflow-hidden"
                    onMouseDown={(e) => startDrag(stem, e)}
                  >
                    <div
                      className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"
                      style={{
                        height: `${(volumes[stem] ?? 1) * 100}%`,
                        background: "linear-gradient(to top, #22C55E, #4F46E5)",
                      }}
                    ></div>
                    <span className="relative text-xs text-white font-bold mb-1 z-10">
                      {Math.round((volumes[stem] ?? 1) * 100)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


========================================

=== frontend\src\context\AuthContext.jsx ===

import React from "react";
import { createContext, useState, useEffect } from "react";

export const AuthContext = createContext();

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
    const [user, setUser] = useState(() => {
        try {
            const stored = localStorage.getItem("user");
            return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : null;
        } catch {
            return null;
        }
    });

    const login = (email) => {
        const newUser = { email };
        setUser(newUser);
        localStorage.setItem("user", JSON.stringify(newUser));
    };

    const logout = () => {
        setUser(null);
        localStorage.removeItem("user");
    };

    return (
        <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, login, logout }}>
            {children}
        </AuthContext.Provider>
    );
}

=== INSTRUCTIONS.md ===


# EchoMind - Running & Testing Guide

## 🚀 Running the Application (Development)
1. **Activate your virtual environment**:
   ```bash
   # Windows
   venv\Scripts\activate
   # Mac/Linux
   source venv/bin/activate
   ```

2. **Start the Flask backend**:
   ```bash
   cd backend
   python app.py
   ```

3. **Start the React frontend**:
   ```bash
   cd frontend
   npm run dev
   ```

4. **Open your browser at the React URL** (usually):
   ```
   http://localhost:5173
   ```

✅ React handles the UI, Flask handles API routes (`/upload`, `/stream/<job_id>`, `/download/<job_id>`). Both must run at the same time in development.

---

## ✅ Running Automated Tests (Backend)
1. **Enable Test Mode**:
   ```powershell
   # Windows PowerShell
   set ECHO_TEST_MODE=1
   # Mac/Linux
   export ECHO_TEST_MODE=1
   ```

2. **Start the backend in test mode**:
   ```bash
   python app.py
   ```

3. **In another terminal, run the tester**:
   ```bash
   python tests/test_backend_v2.py
   ```

4. **View reports** in `tests/reports/`.

---

## 🛠 Deployment (Production)
1. **Install backend dependencies**:
   ```bash
   pip install -r requirements.txt
   ```

2. **Build React frontend**:
   ```bash
   cd frontend
   npm run build
   ```

3. **Serve built files with Flask or Nginx**:
   - Copy `frontend/dist` into a static folder or configure Flask to serve it.
   - Start backend with Gunicorn:
     ```bash
     gunicorn -w 4 app:app
     ```

4. (Optional) Use Nginx as a reverse proxy for SSL and static assets.

---

## 📂 Project Structure
```
EchoMind/
  backend/
    app.py              # Main Flask backend
    audio_splitter.py   # Demucs audio splitting logic
    requirements.txt    # Backend dependencies
    logs/               # Backend logs (contents ignored in snapshots)
    temp/               # Temporary job files
    uploads/            # Uploaded files
  frontend/
    index.html          # Vite React entry point
    package.json        # Frontend dependencies
    tailwind.config.js  # Tailwind setup
    src/
      api.js            # Axios API calls
      App.jsx           # Main React component/UI logic
      main.jsx          # React entry point
      index.css         # Global styles
  tests/
    test_backend_v2.py  # Automated backend tester
    reports/            # Test reports (contents ignored in snapshots)
```

---

## 🔑 Notes:
- `@cross_origin()` is enabled on `/upload`, `/stream`, `/download` routes for React ↔ Flask communication.
- React URL (`localhost:5173`) and Flask URL (`127.0.0.1:5000`) must both be running during development.
- For production, React is built and served from Flask or a reverse proxy like Nginx.
- `logs/`, `temp/`, and `tests/reports/` are kept for context but their file contents are ignored in snapshots.

=== snapshot_project.py ===

import os

BASE_DIR = os.getcwd()
OUTPUT_FILE = "clean_snapshot.txt"

# Core files to always include fully
# Core files to always include fully
CORE_FILES = {
    # Backend core
    "backend/app.py",
    "backend/audio_splitter.py",
    "backend/requirements.txt",
    "backend/db/init_db.py",        # DB init script
    "backend/db/users.db",          # SQLite DB file
    "backend/test.mp3",             # Test media (so I see what you’re using)

    # Frontend core
    "frontend/src/api.js",
    "frontend/src/App.jsx",
    "frontend/src/main.jsx",
    "frontend/src/index.css",
    "frontend/src/components/Navbar.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Hero.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/UploadForm.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Faq.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Login.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Signup.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/Dashboard.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/ResetPasswordRequest.jsx",
    "frontend/src/components/ResetPasswordConfirm.jsx",
    "frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx",
    "frontend/index.html",
    "frontend/package.json",
    "frontend/tailwind.config.js",
    "frontend/postcss.config.js",

    # Project meta
    "INSTRUCTIONS.md",
    "packages_list.txt",
    ".env",

    # Utility / scripts
    "snapshot_project.py",
    "clean_snapshot.txt"
}


# Folders to show but NOT list contents
SKIP_LISTING = {
    os.path.join("backend", "logs"),
    os.path.join("backend", "temp"),
    os.path.join("backend", "uploads"),
    os.path.join("tests", "reports")
}

IGNORE_DIRS = {"node_modules", "venv", "__pycache__"}

with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
    out.write("# 📂 Project Structure\n")

    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(BASE_DIR):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
        rel_root = os.path.relpath(root, BASE_DIR)

        out.write(f"\n# 📁 {rel_root}\n")

        if any(rel_root.startswith(skip) for skip in SKIP_LISTING):
            out.write("  [Contents skipped]\n")
            continue

        for file in files:
            rel_path = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(rel_root, file))
            rel_path_unix = rel_path.replace("\\", "/")

            if rel_path_unix in CORE_FILES:
                out.write(f"\n## 📄 {rel_path_unix}\n")
                try:
                    with open(os.path.join(root, file), "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        out.write(f.read())
                except:
                    out.write("[Unreadable file]")
                out.write("\n" + "-"*60 + "\n")
            else:
                out.write(f"  {file}\n")

print(f"✅ Clean snapshot created: {OUTPUT_FILE}")

=== tests\test_backend.py ===


import os, time, json, requests, datetime
from pathlib import Path

BASE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:5000"
TEST_FILE = "test.mp3"

# Ensure reports folder exists
Path("tests/reports").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Create dummy audio file if needed
if not os.path.exists(TEST_FILE):
    with open(TEST_FILE, "wb") as f:
        f.write(os.urandom(1024 * 100))  # 100KB placeholder

def upload_file():
    files = {'file': open(TEST_FILE, 'rb')}
    data = {'stems': ['vocals', 'other']}
    r = requests.post(f"{BASE_URL}/upload", files=files, data=data)
    return r.json()

def download_file(job_id):
    r = requests.get(f"{BASE_URL}/download/{job_id}", stream=True)
    return r.status_code

def log_result(results, name, status, detail=""):
    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    results.append({"test": name, "status": status, "detail": detail, "timestamp": timestamp})
    print(f"[{status}] {name} {('- ' + detail) if detail else ''}")

def trigger_cleanup():
    try:
        requests.get(f"{BASE_URL}/internal/cleanup")
    except Exception:
        pass

# Core Tests
def test_valid_upload(results):
    job = upload_file()
    job_id = job.get("job_id")
    log_result(results, "Valid Upload", "PASS" if job_id else "FAIL")

def test_disk_purge(results):
    job = upload_file()
    job_id = job.get("job_id")
    trigger_cleanup()
    log_result(results, "Disk Cap Purge", "PASS" if job_id else "FAIL")

def test_race_download(results):
    job = upload_file()
    job_id = job.get("job_id")
    time.sleep(2)
    download_file(job_id)
    trigger_cleanup()
    log_result(results, "Race Condition Download", "PASS")

def test_flood(results):
    jobs = [upload_file() for _ in range(5)]
    limited = any("error" in j for j in jobs if isinstance(j, dict))
    log_result(results, "Flood Protection", "PASS" if limited else "FAIL")

def test_refresh(results):
    job = upload_file()
    job_id = job.get("job_id")
    time.sleep(2)
    code = download_file(job_id)
    log_result(results, "Refresh Edge Case", "PASS" if code in (200, 404) else "FAIL")

def test_cleanup(results):
    job = upload_file()
    job_id = job.get("job_id")
    trigger_cleanup()
    log_result(results, "Cleanup Verification", "PASS")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    results = []
    tests = [test_valid_upload, test_disk_purge, test_race_download, test_flood, test_refresh, test_cleanup]

    for t in tests:
        try:
            t(results)
        except Exception as e:
            log_result(results, t.__name__, "FAIL", str(e))

    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M-%S")
    report_path = f"tests/reports/test_core_{timestamp}.txt"
    json_path = f"tests/reports/test_core_{timestamp}.json"

    with open(report_path, "w") as f:
        for r in results:
            f.write(f"{r['timestamp']} - {r['test']} - {r['status']} {r['detail']}")

    with open(json_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)

    print(f"\nCore test report saved to {report_path} and {json_path}")
